### [75] [Multimodal Machine Learning for Early Prediction of Metastasis in a Swedish Multi-Cancer Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29793)
*Franco Rugolon,Korbinian Randl,Braslav Jovanovic,Ioanna Miliou,Panagiotis Papapetrou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态机器学习框架，利用电子健康记录中的结构化与非结构化数据，在诊断前一个月预测癌症转移风险，在四种癌症类型上评估不同融合策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态机器学习能够整合电子健康记录中的结构化数据（如实验室结果、用药记录）和非结构化数据（如临床文本），为患者状态提供更全面的视图。当前需要开发能够在癌症诊断前预测转移风险的预测模型，以支持早期干预和个性化治疗。

Method: 使用来自卡罗林斯卡大学医院的四种癌症队列数据（乳腺癌743例、结肠癌387例、肺癌870例、前列腺癌1890例），包含人口统计学、合并症、实验室结果、用药和临床文本。采用TRIPOD 2a设计，80-20开发-验证分割。比较传统和深度学习分类器在单模态和多模态组合上的性能，使用多种融合策略（包括中间融合）。评估指标包括AUROC、AUPRC、F1分数、敏感性和特异性。采用多模态SHAP适应方法分析分类器推理。

Result: 中间融合在乳腺癌（F1=0.845）、结肠癌（F1=0.786）和前列腺癌（F1=0.845）上获得最高F1分数。对于肺癌，中间融合F1为0.819，而仅文本模型获得最高F1=0.829。深度学习分类器始终优于传统模型。最小的结肠癌队列性能最低，突显足够训练数据的重要性。SHAP分析显示不同癌症类型中模态的相对重要性存在差异。

Conclusion: 融合策略各有优缺点，中间融合通常能提供最佳结果，但策略选择应根据数据特征和组织需求进行调整。多模态方法能够有效整合电子健康记录中的异构数据，为癌症转移风险预测提供有力工具，深度学习模型在此任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Machine Learning offers a holistic view of a patient's status, integrating structured and unstructured data from electronic health records (EHR). We propose a framework to predict metastasis risk one month prior to diagnosis, using six months of clinical history from EHR data. Data from four cancer cohorts collected at Karolinska University Hospital (Stockholm, Sweden) were analyzed: breast (n = 743), colon (n = 387), lung (n = 870), and prostate (n = 1890). The dataset included demographics, comorbidities, laboratory results, medications, and clinical text. We compared traditional and deep learning classifiers across single modalities and multimodal combinations, using various fusion strategies and a Transparent Reporting of a multivariable prediction model for Individual Prognosis Or Diagnosis (TRIPOD) 2a design, with an 80-20 development-validation split to ensure a rigorous, repeatable evaluation. Performance was evaluated using AUROC, AUPRC, F1 score, sensitivity, and specificity. We then employed a multimodal adaptation of SHAP to analyze the classifiers' reasoning. Intermediate fusion achieved the highest F1 scores on breast (0.845), colon (0.786), and prostate cancer (0.845), demonstrating strong predictive performance. For lung cancer, the intermediate fusion achieved an F1 score of 0.819, while the text-only model achieved the highest, with an F1 score of 0.829. Deep learning classifiers consistently outperformed traditional models. Colon cancer, the smallest cohort, had the lowest performance, highlighting the importance of sufficient training data. SHAP analysis showed that the relative importance of modalities varied across cancer types. Fusion strategies offer distinct strengths and weaknesses. Intermediate fusion consistently delivered the best results, but strategy choices should align with data characteristics and organizational needs.

</details>


### [76] [AMShortcut: An Inference- and Training-Efficient Inverse Design Model for Amorphous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29812)
*Yan Lin,Jonas A. Finkler,Tao Du,Jilin Hu,Morten M. Smedskjaer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMShortcut是一种用于非晶材料逆设计的概率生成模型，通过少量采样步骤实现高效推理，并能基于任意属性组合进行条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶材料缺乏长程原子序但具有复杂的短程和中程序，需要大尺寸模拟单元（数百至数千原子）。传统方法需要大量采样步骤且针对不同属性组合需分别训练模型，效率低下。需要开发既能高效推理又能灵活处理多种属性组合的生成模型。

Method: 提出AMShortcut概率生成模型，采用推理和训练高效的架构。模型通过少量采样步骤即可准确推断非晶材料的多样短程和中程结构，避免过多采样步骤对推理效率的影响。模型可一次性训练包含所有相关属性，并能基于任意所需属性组合进行条件推理。

Result: 在三个具有不同结构和属性的非晶材料数据集上的实验表明，AMShortcut实现了其设计目标：高效推理（少量采样步骤）和灵活的条件生成（基于任意属性组合）。

Conclusion: AMShortcut为非晶材料逆设计提供了一种高效的概率生成方法，能够以少量采样步骤生成具有所需属性的非晶材料结构，有望促进非晶材料在能源存储和热管理等领域的应用。

Abstract: Amorphous materials are solids that lack long-range atomic order but possess complex short- and medium-range order. Unlike crystalline materials that can be described by unit cells containing few up to hundreds of atoms, amorphous materials require larger simulation cells with at least hundreds or often thousands of atoms. Inverse design of amorphous materials with probabilistic generative models aims to generate the atomic positions and elements of amorphous materials given a set of desired properties. It has emerged as a promising approach for facilitating the application of amorphous materials in domains such as energy storage and thermal management. In this paper, we introduce AMShortcut, an inference- and training-efficient probabilistic generative model for amorphous materials. AMShortcut enables accurate inference of diverse short- and medium-range structures in amorphous materials with only a few sampling steps, mitigating the need for an excessive number of sampling steps that hinders inference efficiency. AMShortcut can be trained once with all relevant properties and perform inference conditioned on arbitrary combinations of desired properties, mitigating the need for training one model for each combination. Experiments on three amorphous materials datasets with diverse structures and properties demonstrate that AMShortcut achieves its design goals.

</details>


### [77] [Loss Gap Parity for Fairness in Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29818)
*Brahim Erraji,Michaël Perrot,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EAGLE是一种联邦学习算法，通过正则化全局模型来最小化客户端间损失差距的差异，实现相对改进的公平性，特别适用于异构环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，客户端期望全局模型在自身数据上表现良好，但存在异构性导致不同客户端的最优本地模型不一致。现有方法追求损失平等可能损害多数客户端性能，需要一种能确保所有客户端获得相似损失差距（全局模型与最优本地模型性能差异）的方法。

Method: 提出EAGLE算法，通过显式正则化全局模型来最小化客户端间损失差距的差异。算法优先关注距离其本地最优损失最远的客户端，确保相对改进的公平性而非绝对损失平等。

Result: 理论分析：在非凸损失函数下提供收敛保证，并使用新的异构性度量描述算法迭代相对于标准联邦学习目标的性能。实证结果：EAGLE显著减少客户端间损失差距的差异，同时在凸和非凸情况下保持与强基线相当的实用性。

Conclusion: EAGLE通过关注相对改进公平性而非绝对损失平等，有效解决了联邦学习中的异构性问题，在减少客户端间性能差异的同时保持了整体实用性，为联邦学习中的公平性提供了新视角。

Abstract: While clients may join federated learning to improve performance on data they rarely observe locally, they often remain self-interested, expecting the global model to perform well on their own data. This motivates an objective that ensures all clients achieve a similar loss gap -the difference in performance between the global model and the best model they could train using only their local data-. To this end, we propose EAGLE, a novel federated learning algorithm that explicitly regularizes the global model to minimize disparities in loss gaps across clients. Our approach is particularly effective in heterogeneous settings, where the optimal local models of the clients may be misaligned. Unlike existing methods that encourage loss parity, potentially degrading performance for many clients, EAGLE targets fairness in relative improvements. We provide theoretical convergence guarantees for EAGLE under non-convex loss functions, and characterize how its iterates perform relative to the standard federated learning objective using a novel heterogeneity measure. Empirically, we demonstrate that EAGLE reduces the disparity in loss gaps among clients by prioritizing those furthest from their local optimal loss, while maintaining competitive utility in both convex and non-convex cases compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Curvature-Guided LoRA: Steering in the pretrained NTK subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29824)
*Frédéric Zheng,Alexandre Proutière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CG-LoRA：一种基于曲率信息的预测对齐方法，通过二阶优化提升LoRA性能，使其更接近全参数微调效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）性能通常不及全参数微调，因为它们主要关注参数更新对齐，而非直接控制模型预测输出

Method: 提出预测对齐问题，将PEFT获得的预测器与全参数微调的预测器在输出层面进行匹配；推导出曲率感知的二阶公式，提出CG-LoRA方法，利用局部曲率信息选择和缩放适应方向

Result: 在标准自然语言理解基准测试中，相比现有LoRA变体，CG-LoRA表现出更好的性能和更快的收敛速度

Conclusion: 通过预测对齐和曲率引导的优化策略，CG-LoRA能够有效提升参数高效微调方法的性能，使其更接近全参数微调效果，同时保持计算效率

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA enable efficient adaptation of large pretrained models but often fall short of full fine-tuning performance. Existing approaches focus on aligning parameter updates, which only indirectly control model predictions. In this work, we introduce the prediction alignment problem, aiming to match the predictor obtained via PEFT to that of full fine-tuning at the level of outputs. We show that this objective naturally leads to a curvature-aware, second-order formulation, where optimal low-rank updates correspond to a Newton-like, curvature-whitened gradient. Based on this insight, we propose Curvature-Guided LoRA (CG-LoRA), which selects and scales adaptation directions using local curvature information. Our method is computationally efficient and avoids explicit second-order matrix construction. Preliminary experiments on standard natural language understanding benchmarks demonstrate improved performance and faster convergence compared to existing LoRA variants.

</details>


### [79] [Task Scarcity and Label Leakage in Relational Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29914)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures,Denis Oliveira Correa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出K-Space架构，通过梯度投影方法抑制标签泄漏，提升关系基础模型在相同数据库内的任务迁移能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系基础模型训练需要学习跨任务可迁移的表征，但可用监督通常局限于每个数据库的少量预测目标。这种任务稀缺性导致学习到的表征编码了任务特定的捷径，即使在相同模式内也会降低迁移能力，作者称之为标签泄漏问题。

Method: 提出K-Space架构：结合冻结的预训练表格编码器和轻量级消息传递核心。为抑制标签泄漏，引入梯度投影方法，从表征更新中移除标签预测方向。

Result: 在RelBench基准测试中，该方法将相同数据集内的迁移性能平均提升了+0.145 AUROC，通常能恢复接近单任务性能的水平。

Conclusion: 结果表明，限制关系基础模型性能的因素不仅是有限的数据，还包括有限的任务多样性。梯度投影方法能有效缓解标签泄漏问题，提升模型在相同模式内的任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Training relational foundation models requires learning representations that transfer across tasks, yet available supervision is typically limited to a small number of prediction targets per database. This task scarcity causes learned representations to encode task-specific shortcuts that degrade transfer even within the same schema, a problem we call label leakage. We study this using K-Space, a modular architecture combining frozen pretrained tabular encoders with a lightweight message-passing core. To suppress leakage, we introduce a gradient projection method that removes label-predictive directions from representation updates. On RelBench, this improves within-dataset transfer by +0.145 AUROC on average, often recovering near single-task performance. Our results suggest that limited task diversity, not just limited data, constrains relational foundation models.

</details>


### [80] [Real-Time Explanations for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29946)
*Luan Borges Teodoro Reis Sena,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShapPFN：一种集成Shapley值回归到架构中的基础模型，单次前向传播即可同时生成预测和解释，比KernelSHAP快1000倍以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性对科学机器学习至关重要，但现有解释方法（如SHAP）计算成本高，限制了交互式探索。需要一种能高效生成高质量解释的模型。

Method: 提出ShapPFN基础模型，将Shapley值回归直接集成到模型架构中，通过单次前向传播同时产生预测和解释。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，ShapPFN达到竞争性性能，同时产生高保真度解释（R²=0.96，余弦相似度=0.99），比KernelSHAP快1000倍以上（0.06秒 vs 610秒）。

Conclusion: ShapPFN通过将解释生成集成到模型架构中，实现了高效、高质量的可解释性，为科学机器学习的交互式探索提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Interpretability is central for scientific machine learning, as understanding \emph{why} models make predictions enables hypothesis generation and validation. While tabular foundation models show strong performance, existing explanation methods like SHAP are computationally expensive, limiting interactive exploration. We introduce ShapPFN, a foundation model that integrates Shapley value regression directly into its architecture, producing both predictions and explanations in a single forward pass. On standard benchmarks, ShapPFN achieves competitive performance while producing high-fidelity explanations ($R^2$=0.96, cosine=0.99) over 1000\times faster than KernelSHAP (0.06s vs 610s). Our code is available at https://github.com/kunumi/ShapPFN

</details>


### [81] [Reward-Based Online LLM Routing via NeuralUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30035)
*Ming-Hua Tsai,Phat Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索了使用NeuralUCB进行成本感知的大型语言模型路由，在模拟在线设置中相比随机和最小成本基线获得更高的效用奖励，同时比最大质量参考显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法主要分为监督路由方法和部分反馈方法，各自在效率和适应性方面存在不同的权衡，需要探索更有效的成本感知路由策略。

Method: 实现基于NeuralUCB的路由策略，并在RouterBench模拟在线设置中进行评估，比较随机、最小成本和最大质量参考等基线方法。

Result: 提出的方法在效用奖励方面持续优于随机和最小成本基线；与最大质量参考相比，在保持竞争性奖励的同时显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: NeuralUCB是成本感知LLM路由的有前景方法，但同时也突显了在动作区分和探索方面仍存在的挑战。

Abstract: This study investigates the use of NeuralUCB for cost-aware large language model (LLM) routing. Existing routing approaches can be broadly grouped into supervised routing methods and partial-feedback methods, each with different tradeoffs in efficiency and adaptivity. We implement a NeuralUCB-based routing policy and evaluate it on RouterBench under a simulated online setting. Experimental results show that the proposed method consistently outperforms random and min-cost baselines in utility reward. Compared with the max-quality reference, our method achieves substantially lower inference cost while maintaining competitive reward. These findings suggest that NeuralUCB is a promising approach for cost-aware LLM routing, while also highlighting remaining challenges in action discrimination and exploration.

</details>


### [82] [Quantifying Cross-Modal Interactions in Multimodal Glioma Survival Prediction via InterSHAP: Evidence for Additive Signal Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29977)
*Iain Swift,JingHua Ye,Ruairi O'Reilly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将Shapley交互指数从分类任务扩展到Cox比例风险模型，用于量化胶质瘤生存预测中的跨模态交互作用，发现预测性能与跨模态交互呈负相关关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态深度学习在癌症预后中通常假设能从跨模态协同交互中获益，但这一假设在生存预测环境中尚未得到直接验证。本研究旨在量化跨模态交互作用，并检验其与预测性能的关系。

Method: 将InterSHAP（基于Shapley交互指数的指标）从分类任务扩展到Cox比例风险模型，应用于胶质瘤生存预测。使用TCGA-GBM和TCGA-LGG数据（n=575），评估四种融合全切片图像（WSI）和RNA-seq特征的架构。

Result: 发现预测性能与测量的交互作用呈反比关系：获得优越判别性能（C-index 0.64→0.82）的架构表现出相等或更低的跨模态交互（4.8%→3.0%）。方差分解显示所有架构中稳定的加性贡献（WSI≈40%，RNA≈55%，交互≈4%）。

Conclusion: 性能提升源于互补信号聚合而非学习到的协同作用。这些发现为比较融合策略提供了实用的模型审计工具，重新构建了架构复杂性在多模态融合中的作用，并对隐私保护的联邦部署具有启示意义。

Abstract: Multimodal deep learning for cancer prognosis is commonly assumed to benefit from synergistic cross-modal interactions, yet this assumption has not been directly tested in survival prediction settings. This work adapts InterSHAP, a Shapley interaction index-based metric, from classification to Cox proportional hazards models and applies it to quantify cross-modal interactions in glioma survival prediction. Using TCGA-GBM and TCGA-LGG data (n=575), we evaluate four fusion architectures combining whole-slide image (WSI) and RNA-seq features. Our central finding is an inverse relationship between predictive performance and measured interaction: architectures achieving superior discrimination (C-index 0.64$\to$0.82) exhibit equivalent or lower cross-modal interaction (4.8\%$\to$3.0\%). Variance decomposition reveals stable additive contributions across all architectures (WSI${\approx}$40\%, RNA${\approx}$55\%, Interaction${\approx}$4\%), indicating that performance gains arise from complementary signal aggregation rather than learned synergy. These findings provide a practical model auditing tool for comparing fusion strategies, reframe the role of architectural complexity in multimodal fusion, and have implications for privacy-preserving federated deployment.

</details>


### [83] [Aligning Validation with Deployment: Target-Weighted Cross-Validation for Spatial Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29981)
*Alexander Brenning,Thomas Suesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标加权交叉验证(TWCV)方法，通过校准加权调整验证损失权重，解决空间预测中验证任务与部署任务分布不匹配导致的预测风险估计偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交叉验证(CV)假设验证任务与部署任务来自相同分布，但在空间预测等结构化数据场景中，这一假设常被违反，导致部署风险估计存在偏差。需要开发能够处理验证与部署任务分布差异的风险估计方法。

Method: 提出目标加权交叉验证(TWCV)：1) 使用任务描述符(如协变量和空间配置)表征预测任务；2) 通过校准加权为验证损失分配权重，使加权的验证任务经验分布与目标域分布匹配；3) 结合空间缓冲重采样，增加部署分布支持的覆盖度，多样化任务难度分布。

Result: 模拟研究中，传统方法和空间估计器在不同采样场景下表现出显著偏差，而缓冲TWCV在所有场景中保持近似无偏。环境污染映射案例研究进一步证实，验证与部署任务分布差异会影响性能评估，缓冲TWCV能更好地反映目标域上的预测任务。

Conclusion: 任务分布不匹配是空间预测中交叉验证偏差的主要来源，校准加权与合适的验证任务生成器相结合，为数据集偏移下的预测风险估计提供了可行方法。TWCV能够处理协变量偏移和任务难度偏移，提高风险估计的准确性。

Abstract: Cross-validation (CV) is commonly used to estimate predictive risk when independent test data are unavailable. Its validity depends on the assumption that validation tasks are sampled from the same distribution as prediction tasks encountered during deployment. In spatial prediction and other settings with structured data, this assumption is frequently violated, leading to biased estimates of deployment risk. We propose Target-Weighted CV (TWCV), an estimator of deployment risk that accounts for discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions, thus accounting for (1) covariate shift and (2) task-difficulty shift. We characterize prediction tasks by descriptors such as covariates and spatial configuration. TWCV assigns weights to validation losses such that the weighted empirical distribution of validation tasks matches the corresponding distribution over a target domain. The weights are obtained via calibration weighting, yielding an importance-weighted estimator that targets deployment risk. Since TWCV requires adequate coverage of the deployment distribution's support, we combine it with spatially buffered resampling that diversifies the task difficulty distribution. In a simulation study, conventional as well as spatial estimators exhibit substantial bias depending on sampling, whereas buffered TWCV remains approximately unbiased across scenarios. A case study in environmental pollution mapping further confirms that discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions can affect performance assessment, and that buffered TWCV better reflects the prediction task over the target domain. These results establish task distribution mismatch as a primary source of CV bias in spatial prediction and show that calibration weighting combined with a suitable validation task generator provides a viable approach to estimating predictive risk under dataset shift.

</details>


### [84] [Refined Detection for Gumbel Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30017)
*Tor Lattimore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对Aaronson(2022)提出的Gumbel水印方案的简单检测机制，该机制在独立同分布假设下被证明是模型无关水印方案中接近最优的


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson(2022)提出的Gumbel水印方案需要有效的检测机制。现有检测方法可能不是最优的，特别是在模型无关的水印方案中，需要一种简单且理论保证的检测方法

Method: 提出了一种简单的检测机制，专门针对Gumbel水印方案。该方法在假设下一个令牌分布是独立同分布采样的条件下，从问题依赖的角度证明了其接近最优性

Result: 新的检测机制被证明在所有模型无关的水印方案中是接近最优的，具有理论保证，并且实现简单

Conclusion: 提出的简单检测机制为Gumbel水印方案提供了理论保证的接近最优检测性能，特别适用于模型无关的水印应用场景

Abstract: We propose a simple detection mechanism for the Gumbel watermarking scheme proposed by Aaronson (2022). The new mechanism is proven to be near-optimal in a problem-dependent sense among all model-agnostic watermarking schemes under the assumption that the next-token distribution is sampled i.i.d.

</details>


### [85] [Tucker Attention: A generalization of approximate attention mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30033)
*Timon Klein,Jonas Kusch,Sebastian Sager,Stefan Schnake,Steffen Schotthöfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tucker Attention，一种基于Tucker分解的参数高效自注意力机制，比GQA和MLA减少一个数量级参数，同时包含MHA、GQA、MLA作为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有减少自注意力内存占用的方法（如GQA、MLA）采用非常规的低秩分解策略，缺乏经典低秩近似理论的解释，需要澄清这些方法实际近似什么对象以及如何解释其低秩行为。

Method: 提出广义的自注意力权重对象视图和基于Tucker分解的因子化策略，构建Tucker Attention参数高效方案，兼容flash-attention和RoPE。

Result: Tucker Attention在LLM和ViT测试中，相比GQA和MLA，达到可比验证指标的同时减少一个数量级参数；揭示了MHA、GQA、MLA的实际秩特性，并简化了MLA。

Conclusion: Tucker Attention提供了统一的自注意力参数化框架，通过经典低秩近似理论解释现有方法，实现参数效率的显著提升，并为未来注意力机制设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: The pursuit of reducing the memory footprint of the self-attention mechanism in multi-headed self attention (MHA) spawned a rich portfolio of methods, e.g., group-query attention (GQA) and multi-head latent attention (MLA). The methods leverage specialized low-rank factorizations across embedding dimensions or attention heads. From the point of view of classical low-rank approximation, these methods are unconventional and raise questions of which objects they really approximate and how to interpret the low-rank behavior of the resulting representations. To answer these questions, this work proposes a generalized view on the weight objects in the self-attention layer and a factorization strategy, which allows us to construct a parameter efficient scheme, called Tucker Attention. Tucker Attention requires an order of magnitude fewer parameters for comparable validation metrics, compared to GQA and MLA, as evaluated in LLM and ViT test cases. Additionally, Tucker Attention~encompasses GQA, MLA, MHA as special cases and is fully compatible with flash-attention and rotary position embeddings (RoPE). This generalization strategy yields insights of the actual ranks achieved by MHA, GQA, and MLA, and further enables simplifications for MLA.

</details>


### [86] [Aligned, Orthogonal or In-conflict: When can we safely optimize Chain-of-Thought?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30036)
*Max Kaufmann,David Lindner,Roland S. Zimmermann,and Rohin Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个预测思维链（CoT）可监控性如何受训练影响的框架，将LLM后训练建模为RL环境，发现当奖励项冲突时会降低CoT可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链监控是监督AI系统的有效方法，但训练过程可能影响CoT的可监控性，例如模型可能学会隐藏关键推理特征。需要理解何时以及为何会发生这种情况。

Method: 提出概念框架，将LLM后训练建模为RL环境，奖励分解为依赖最终输出的项和依赖CoT的项。根据这两项的关系将其分类为"对齐"、"正交"或"冲突"。预测训练对CoT可监控性的影响，并通过在分类环境中训练LLM来验证框架。

Result: 实验验证发现：(1) 使用"冲突"奖励项训练会降低CoT可监控性；(2) 优化冲突奖励项具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够有效预测训练对CoT可监控性的影响，特别是当奖励项冲突时会损害监控能力，这对AI系统监督具有重要意义。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) monitoring, in which automated systems monitor the CoT of an LLM, is a promising approach for effectively overseeing AI systems. However, the extent to which a model's CoT helps us oversee the model - the monitorability of the CoT - can be affected by training, for instance by the model learning to hide important features of its reasoning. We propose and empirically validate a conceptual framework for predicting when and why this occurs. We model LLM post-training as an RL environment where the reward decomposes into two terms: one term depending on final outputs and another term depending on the CoT. Our framework allows us to classify these two terms as "aligned", "orthogonal", or "in-conflict" before training. We predict that training with in-conflict terms will reduce monitorability, orthogonal terms will not affect it, and aligned terms will improve it. To validate our framework, we use it to classify a set of RL environments, train LLMs within those environments, and evaluate how training affects CoT monitorability. We find that (1) training with "in-conflict" reward terms reduces CoT monitorability and (2) optimizing in-conflict reward terms is difficult.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [H Infinity Minimal Destabilizing Feedback for Vulnerability Analysis and Attack Design of Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28891)
*Gavin Glenn,Emma J. Reid*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了鲁棒控制理论中的小增益定理如何既能用于设计稳定系统，也能被攻击者用来设计最小规模的恶意反馈攻击以破坏系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒稳定性问题涉及设计在建模不确定性下仍保持稳定的控制系统。小增益定理用于量化保证稳定性的最大不确定性程度，这些概念被广泛应用于关键基础设施控制系统的设计。然而，同样的概念也可能被攻击者利用来设计最小规模的恶意反馈攻击，以驱动闭环系统失稳。

Method: 首先详细回顾了鲁棒控制中允许构建最小失稳器的结果。然后利用线性扰动理论，在状态空间背景下明确证明：当攻击增益略微增加时，对于一大类非线性系统，内部失稳是保证发生的。

Result: 研究表明，最小规模的攻击仅将系统推到稳定性边界，这在线性化系统失稳的情况下并不一定导致非线性系统失稳。主要结果是：通过线性扰动理论证明，当攻击增益略微增加时，对于一大类非线性系统，内部失稳是保证发生的。

Conclusion: 鲁棒控制理论中的小增益定理具有双重用途：既可用于设计稳定系统，也可被攻击者利用来设计最小规模的破坏性攻击。论文揭示了最小规模攻击的局限性，并证明了通过略微增加攻击增益可以保证非线性系统的内部失稳，这对关键基础设施的安全防护具有重要意义。

Abstract: The robust stability problem involves designing a controlled system which remains stable in the presence of modeling uncertainty. In this context, results known as small gain theorems are used to quantify the maximum amount of uncertainty for which stability is guaranteed. These notions inform the design of numerous control systems, including critical infrastructure components such as power grids, gas pipelines, and water systems. However, these same concepts can be used by an adversary to design a malicious feedback attack, of minimal size, to drive the closed-loop system to instability. In this paper, we first present a detailed review of the results in robust control which allow for the construction of minimal destabilizers. These minimally sized attacks merely push the system to the stability boundary, which we demonstrate do not necessarily destabilize nonlinear systems even when the linearization is destabilized. Our main result leverages linear perturbation theory to explicitly prove, in the state space context, that internal destabilization is guaranteed for a broad class of nonlinear systems when the gain of these attacks is slightly increased.

</details>


### [88] [Symmetrizing Bregman Divergence on the Cone of Positive Definite Matrices: Which Mean to Use and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28917)
*Tushar Sial,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了正定矩阵锥上由一般镜射映射诱导的Bregman散度对称化的变分原理，证明了对称化散度的计算可归结为在满足特定公理性质的均值泛函集上最小化，并确定了不同对称化方式对应的典型均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正定矩阵锥上Bregman散度对称化的理论基础，理解现有文献中对称化实践背后的数学原理，为选择适当的均值提供理论指导。

Method: 采用变分原理方法，将对称化散度的计算形式化为在满足特定公理性质的均值泛函集上的最小化问题。分析前向对称化和反向对称化两种情况，分别证明其对偶空间和原始空间的算术均值作为典型均值的性质。

Result: 对于前向对称化，证明任何镜射映射下原始空间的算术均值都是典型的；对于反向对称化，证明典型均值是对偶空间的算术均值拉回到原始空间。将结果应用于三种常见镜射映射，得到反向对称化的典型均值分别为算术均值、对数欧几里得均值和调和均值。

Conclusion: 该研究为Bregman散度对称化提供了系统的变分原理框架，深化了对现有对称化实践的理解，并为选择适当的均值提供了理论依据，可视为指导均值选择的导航图。

Abstract: This work uncovers variational principles behind symmetrizing the Bregman divergences induced by generic mirror maps over the cone of positive definite matrices. We show that computing the canonical means for this symmetrization can be posed as minimizing the desired symmetrized divergences over a set of mean functionals defined axiomatically to satisfy certain properties. For the forward symmetrization, we prove that the arithmetic mean over the primal space is canonical for any mirror map over the positive definite cone. For the reverse symmetrization, we show that the canonical mean is the arithmetic mean over the dual space, pulled back to the primal space. Applying this result to three common mirror maps used in practice, we show that the canonical means for reverse symmetrization, in those cases, turn out to be the arithmetic, log-Euclidean and harmonic means. Our results improve understanding of existing symmetrization practices in the literature, and can be seen as a navigational chart to help decide which mean to use when.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond binarity: Semidefinite programming for ternary quadratic problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28979)
*Frank de Meijer,Veronica Piccialli,Renata Sotirov,Antonio M. Sudoso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对三元二次规划问题(TQP)提出首个整数半定规划框架，开发了包含有效不等式和分支定界的精确求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然半定规划技术已成熟应用于{0,1}和{±1}值二次规划问题，但针对三元值{0, ±1}的二次优化问题尚无专门的整数半定规划框架。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出三元SDP公式化；2) 推导秩一三元正半定矩阵的新理论见解；3) 构建基础SDP松弛并加强(三角形、RLT、分割和k边形不等式)；4) 设计定制分支定界算法，迭代求解加强SDP、分离违反不等式、应用三元分支策略并计算高质量可行解。

Result: 在受实践启发的TQP变体(无约束、线性约束和二次比率问题)上进行计算测试，结果表明所提算法具有有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为三元二次规划问题建立了首个整数半定规划框架，通过理论创新和算法设计实现了有效的精确求解方法。

Abstract: We study the ternary quadratic problem (TQP), a quadratic optimization problem with linear constraints where the variables take values in $\{0, \pm 1\}$. While semidefinite programming (SDP) techniques are well established for $\{0,1\}$- and $\{\pm 1\}$-valued quadratic problems, no dedicated integer semidefinite programming framework exists for the ternary case. In this paper, we introduce a ternary SDP formulation for the TQP that forms the basis of an exact solution approach. We derive new theoretical insights in rank-one ternary positive semidefinite matrices, which lead to a basic SDP relaxation that is further strengthened by valid triangle, RLT, split and $k$-gonal inequalities. These are embedded in a tailored branch-and-bound algorithm that iteratively solves strengthened SDPs, separates violated inequalities, applies a ternary branching strategy and computes high-quality feasible solutions. We test our algorithm on TQP variations motivated by practice, including unconstrained, linearly constrained and quadratic ratio problems. Computational results on these instances demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [90] [Multi-fidelity approaches for general constrained Bayesian optimization with application to aircraft design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28987)
*Oihan Cordelier,Youssef Diouane,Nathalie Bartoli,Eric Laurendeau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的多保真度贝叶斯优化策略，在飞机设计优化中同时考虑目标和约束信息，相比现有方法在有限计算预算下能获得86%到200%更多满足约束的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机设计中的多学科设计优化问题计算成本高昂，现有多保真度模型仅关注目标函数的信息融合，未能充分利用约束信息来进一步降低计算成本。

Method: 提出新颖的多保真度选择策略，同时整合目标和约束的信息来指导保真度选择；使用线性涡格法进行气动分析，有限元法进行结构分析；在四个分析测试案例和飞机机翼气动结构设计问题上验证方法。

Result: 在有限计算预算下，相比最先进方法，提出的多保真度方法能获得86%到200%更多满足约束的解，显著提高了优化效率。

Conclusion: 同时利用目标和约束信息的多保真度选择策略能显著降低计算成本而不影响解的最优性，为飞机设计等复杂多学科优化问题提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Aircraft design relies heavily on solving challenging and computationally expensive Multidisciplinary Design Optimization problems. In this context, there has been growing interest in multi-fidelity models for Bayesian optimization to improve the MDO process by balancing computational cost and accuracy through the combination of high- and low-fidelity simulation models, enabling efficient exploration of the design process at a minimal computational effort. In the existing literature, fidelity selection focuses only on the objective function to decide how to integrate multiple fidelity levels, balancing precision and computational cost using variance reduction criteria. In this work, we propose novel multi-fidelity selection strategies. Specifically, we demonstrate how incorporating information from both the objective and the constraints can further reduce computational costs without compromising the optimality of the solution. We validate the proposed multi-fidelity optimization strategy by applying it to four analytical test cases, showcasing its effectiveness. The proposed method is used to efficiently solve a challenging aircraft wing aero-structural design problem. The proposed setting uses a linear vortex lattice method and a finite element method for the aerodynamic and structural analysis respectively. We show that employing our proposed multi-fidelity approach leads to $86\%$ to $200\%$ more constraint compliant solutions given a limited budget compared to the state-of-the-art approach.

</details>


### [91] [Collisionless Multi-Agent Path Planning in the Hamilton-Jacobi Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28993)
*Christian Parkinson,Adan Baca,Huy Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的无碰撞多智能体路径规划方法，无需分层规划器，支持异构智能体和高维动力学


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体路径规划方法通常需要分层规划器或黑盒组件，难以处理异构智能体和高维动力学系统。本文旨在开发基于最优控制理论和偏微分方程的通用方法，避免这些限制。

Method: 基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程构建无碰撞多智能体路径规划框架，采用变分公式求解该偏微分方程，开发无网格数值方法，使用原始-对偶混合梯度优化方案计算最优轨迹。

Result: 方法在简单汽车和四旋翼无人机路径规划问题上验证有效，能够处理异构智能体和高维动力学，无需分层规划器或黑盒组件。

Conclusion: 基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的方法为多智能体路径规划提供了理论严谨的框架，能够处理复杂动力学和异构系统，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a method for collisionless multi-agent path planning using the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. Because the method is rooted in optimal control theory and partial differential equations, it avoids the need for hierarchical planners and is black-box free. Our model can account for heterogeneous agents and realistic, high-dimensional dynamics. We develop a grid-free numerical method based on a variational formulation of the solution of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation which can resolve optimal trajectories even in high-dimensional problems, and include some practical implementation notes. In particular, we resolve the solution using a primal-dual hybrid gradient optimization scheme. We demonstrate the method's efficacy on path planning problems involving simple cars and quadcopter drones.

</details>


### [92] [A user-driven pricing and scheduling framework for public electric vehicle charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29044)
*Fangting Zhou,Jiaming Wu,Balazs Kulcsar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出用户驱动的电动汽车公共充电定价与调度框架，通过用户投标、运营商决策、用户接受三阶段互动，协调异质用户需求与有限容量，提升系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共充电基础设施利用率不均且低效，运营商定价方案灵活性有限，难以协调异质用户需求与容量约束，需要更灵活的用户驱动定价机制。

Method: 三阶段交互框架：1) 用户提交投标（可接受时段、投标价格、数量边界）；2) 运营商基于投标确定价格和充电时段分配；3) 用户观察结果后决定是否接受。运营商决策考虑利润目标、用户参与要求和跨充电等级与时段的容量约束。

Result: 数值案例研究揭示了不同充电等级下用户接受度、运营商收入和充电容量利用率之间的权衡关系，为设计灵活定价方案提供实践指导。

Conclusion: 该用户驱动定价框架能更好地适应异质用户偏好，提高系统效率，为设计灵活的公共充电定价方案提供实用见解和指导。

Abstract: Public electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure has expanded rapidly, yet utilization across charging stations remains uneven and often inefficient. Existing operator-determined pricing schemes offer limited flexibility to coordinate heterogeneous user demand within constrained capacity. This study proposes a user-driven pricing and scheduling framework for public EV charging. Users submit advance bids specifying acceptable time slots, bid prices, and quantity bounds. Based on these bids, the charging operator determines prices and charging slot assignments. After observing the outcomes, users decide whether to accept the resulting prices and allocations. The operator's decision problem incorporates profit objectives, user participation requirements, and capacity constraints across charging levels and time slots. The framework captures a three-stage interaction involving user bidding, operator decisions, and user acceptance. Numerical case studies reveal trade-offs among user acceptance, operator revenue, and charging capacity utilization under different charging levels. The findings provide practical guidance and insights into designing flexible pricing schemes that better accommodate heterogeneous user preferences while improving system efficiency.

</details>


### [93] [Scalable Co-Design via Linear Design Problems: Compositional Theory and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29083)
*Yubo Cai,Yujun Huang,Meshal Alharbi,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了线性设计问题(LDPs)作为单调协同设计的一个可计算子类，将设计问题转化为多目标线性规划，并开发了精确计算和近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调协同设计虽然提供了复杂工程系统设计的组合语言，但其一般性并未揭示哪些问题类别允许精确且可扩展的计算。需要识别出这样的可计算类别。

Method: 引入线性设计问题(LDPs)，其可行功能-资源关系是欧几里得偏序集上的多面体。证明LDP查询可精确简化为多目标线性规划，并开发了两种构造方法：保持块对角稀疏性的整体提升公式和通过多面体投影逐步消除内部变量的组合公式。

Result: LDPs在基本协同设计互联下是封闭的，任何线性组件的互联都会诱导出系统级LDP。对于凸协同设计资源查询，可以构造任意精度的多面体外近似，且对于标准非负资源锥，衰退锥误差为零。

Conclusion: 线性设计问题为单调协同设计提供了一个可精确计算且可扩展的子类，通过多目标线性规划连接了协同设计语义与多面体多目标优化，为复杂工程系统的设计提供了有效的计算框架。

Abstract: Designing complex engineered systems requires managing tightly coupled trade-offs between subsystem capabilities and resource requirements. Monotone co-design provides a compositional language for such problems, but its generality does not by itself reveal which problem classes admit exact and scalable computation. This paper isolates such a class by introducing Linear Design Problems (LDPs): design problems whose feasible functionality--resource relations are polyhedra over Euclidean posets. We show that queries on LDPs reduce exactly to Multi-Objective Linear Programs (MOLPs), thereby connecting monotone co-design semantics with polyhedral multiobjective optimization. We further prove that LDPs are closed under the fundamental co-design interconnections, implying that any interconnection of linear components induces a system-level LDP. To compute the resulting feasible sets, we develop two complementary constructions: a monolithic lifted formulation that preserves block-angular sparsity, and a compositional formulation that incrementally eliminates internal variables through polyhedral projection. Beyond the exact linear setting, we show that convex co-design resource queries admit arbitrarily accurate polyhedral outer approximations, with recession-cone error identically zero for standard nonnegative resource cones. Numerical studies on synthetic series-chain benchmarks, a gripper, and a rover co-design validate the theory.

</details>


### [94] [Primal-dual dynamics featuring Hessian-driven damping and variable mass for convex optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29124)
*Xiangkai Sun,Feng Guo,Liang He,Xiaole Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种具有变质量和Hessian驱动阻尼的Tikhonov正则化原始-对偶动力系统，用于求解带线性等式约束的凸优化问题，证明轨迹强收敛到最小范数解，并获得收敛速率结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决带线性等式约束的凸优化问题，需要设计能够收敛到最小范数解的动力系统。现有方法在收敛速率和参数调整方面存在局限，因此需要开发具有变质量、Hessian驱动阻尼和Tikhonov正则化的新型动力系统，以改善收敛性能。

Method: 提出一种新的Tikhonov正则化原始-对偶动力系统，包含变质量、慢粘性阻尼、外推和时间尺度等多个时变参数。系统采用Hessian驱动阻尼，通过Lyapunov分析方法研究系统的收敛性质。

Result: 证明了系统轨迹强收敛到优化问题的最小范数解。获得了原始-对偶间隙、目标函数残差和可行性违反的收敛速率。通过适当调整参数可以改善这些收敛速率。数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的具有变质量和Hessian驱动阻尼的Tikhonov正则化原始-对偶动力系统能够有效求解带线性等式约束的凸优化问题，收敛到最小范数解，且通过参数调整可以优化收敛性能。

Abstract: This paper deals with a new Tikhonov regularized primal-dual dynamical system with variable mass and Hessian-driven damping for solving a convex optimization problem with linear equality constraints. The system features several time-dependent parameters: variable mass, slow viscous damping, extrapolation, and temporal scaling. By employing the Lyapunov analysis approach, we obtain the strong convergence of the trajectory generated by the proposed system to the minimal norm solution of the optimization problem, as well as convergence rate results for the primal-dual gap, the objective residual, and the feasibility violation. We also show that the convergence rates of the primal-dual gap, the objective residual, and the feasibility violation can be improved by appropriately adjusting these parameters. Further, we conduct numerical experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of the theoretical results.

</details>


### [95] [Adaptive Delayed-Update Cyclic Algorithm for Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29128)
*Yi Wei,Xufeng Cai,Jelena Diakonikolas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ADUCA是一种自适应延迟更新的循环算法，用于解决单调Lipschitz算子的Minty变分不等式问题，无需参数调优，具有分布式兼容性，达到接近最优的全局复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环块坐标方法是实践中广泛使用的一阶算法，但其理论行为难以解释，步长设置通常需要精心调优或线搜索机制。本文旨在开发一种无需参数调优的循环算法，解决单调Lipschitz算子的Minty变分不等式问题。

Method: 提出ADUCA算法，采用自适应延迟更新机制，无需全局或块级Lipschitz常数，除初始化外无需每轮线搜索。关键特征是使用延迟一个完整周期的算子信息，这使得算法兼容并行和分布式实现，并减少了块间的同步需求。

Result: ADUCA在目标误差ε>0的函数上达到（接近）最优的全局oracle复杂度：对于单调算子，复杂度为1/ε；对于强单调算子，复杂度为log²(1/ε)。

Conclusion: ADUCA是一种参数自由的循环算法，能够有效解决单调Lipschitz算子的Minty变分不等式问题，具有分布式兼容性和接近最优的理论复杂度保证。

Abstract: Cyclic block coordinate methods are a fundamental class of first-order algorithms, widely used in practice for their simplicity and strong empirical performance. Yet, their theoretical behavior remains challenging to explain, and setting their step sizes -- beyond classical coordinate descent for minimization -- typically requires careful tuning or line-search machinery. In this work, we develop $\texttt{ADUCA}$ (Adaptive Delayed-Update Cyclic Algorithm), a cyclic algorithm addressing a broad class of Minty variational inequalities with monotone Lipschitz operators. $\texttt{ADUCA}$ is parameter-free: it requires no global or block-wise Lipschitz constants and uses no per-epoch line search, except at initialization. A key feature of the algorithm is using operator information delayed by a full cycle, which makes the algorithm compatible with parallel and distributed implementations, and attractive due to weakened synchronization requirements across blocks. We prove that $\texttt{ADUCA}$ attains (near) optimal global oracle complexity as a function of target error $ε>0,$ scaling with $1/ε$ for monotone operators, or with $\log^2(1/ε)$ for operators that are strongly monotone.

</details>


### [96] [QOCO-GPU: A Quadratic Objective Conic Optimizer with GPU Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29197)
*Govind M. Chari,Behçet Açıkmeşe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 开发了QOCO求解器的GPU加速后端，使用cuDSS进行稀疏LDL分解，通过CUDA内核优化锥操作，实现50-70倍加速，支持Python和CVXPY接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QOCO求解器基于CPU实现，在处理大规模二次规划和二阶锥规划问题时性能有限，需要利用GPU并行计算能力加速求解过程，特别是针对包含数千万到数亿非零元素的KKT系统。

Method: 1) 使用NVIDIA cuDSS库对KKT系统进行稀疏LDL直接分解；2) 开发自定义CUDA内核优化锥操作；3) 重构QOCO引入模块化后端抽象，解耦求解逻辑与线性代数实现；4) 提供Python和CVXPY接口。

Result: 在包含数千万到数亿非零元素的大规模二次规划和二阶锥规划问题上，GPU后端相比CPU实现实现了50-70倍的加速，显著提升了求解效率。

Conclusion: GPU加速的QOCO后端通过cuDSS稀疏分解和自定义CUDA内核有效利用了GPU并行计算能力，模块化设计保持了代码统一性，为大规模优化问题提供了高效的求解方案。

Abstract: We present a GPU-accelerated backend for QOCO, a C-based solver for quadratic objective second-order cone programs (SOCPs) based on a primal-dual interior point method. Our backend uses NVIDIA's cuDSS library to perform a direct sparse LDL factorization of the KKT system at each iteration. We also develop custom CUDA kernels for cone operations and show that parallelizing these operations is essential for achieving peak performance. Additionally, we refactor QOCO to introduce a modular backend abstraction that decouples solver logic from the underlying linear algebra implementations, allowing the existing CPU and new GPU backend to share a unified codebase. This GPU backend is accessible through a direct Python interface and through CVXPY, allowing for easy use. Numerical experiments on a range of large-scale quadratic programs and SOCPs with tens to hundreds of millions of nonzero elements in the KKT matrix, demonstrate speedups of up to 50-70 times over the CPU implementation.

</details>


### [97] [Pointwise and dynamic programming control synthesis for finite-level open quantum memory systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29225)
*Igor G. Vladimirov,Ian R. Petersen,Guodong Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限级量子存储系统在外部量子噪声下保持初始动态变量的能力，通过经典控制信号优化存储性能，提出两种控制设计方法：点态控制和有限时域动态规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在存在外部量子噪声的情况下，通过经典控制信号优化有限级量子存储系统的性能，以保持系统变量的初始值，这对于量子信息处理和量子存储应用具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 系统变量具有类似泡利矩阵的代数结构，其海森堡图像演化由拟线性量子随机微分方程描述；2. 哈密顿量参数通过确定性时间函数的经典控制信号进行仿射依赖；3. 存储性能用量子系统变量与初始条件的均方偏差量化；4. 提出点态控制设计，最小化均方偏差的时间导数并附加控制二次惩罚；5. 在有限时域设置中应用动态规划，得到二次非线性Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，并通过递归计算的渐近展开求解。

Result: 1. 将存储性能泛函与辅助经典控制仿射动力系统的矩阵值状态相关联；2. 开发了点态控制设计方法，控制信号实时优化存储性能；3. 在有限时域设置中，通过动态规划获得了可求解的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，并提出了递归渐近展开的求解方案。

Conclusion: 该论文为有限级量子存储系统的控制设计提供了系统性的理论框架，通过经典控制信号有效对抗量子噪声对存储变量的影响，点态控制和有限时域动态规划两种方法为量子存储性能优化提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with finite-level quantum memory systems for retaining initial dynamic variables in the presence of external quantum noise. The system variables have an algebraic structure, similar to that of the Pauli matrices, and their Heisenberg picture evolution is governed by a quasilinear quantum stochastic differential equation. The latter involves a Hamiltonian whose parameters depend affinely on a classical control signal in the form of a deterministic function of time. The memory performance is quantified by a mean-square deviation of quantum system variables of interest from their initial conditions. We relate this functional to a matrix-valued state of an auxiliary classical control-affine dynamical system. This leads to a pointwise control design where the control signal minimises the time-derivative of the mean-square deviation with an additional quadratic penalty on the control. In an alternative finite-horizon setting with a terminal-integral cost functional, we apply dynamic programming and obtain a quadratically nonlinear Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, for which a solution is outlined in the form of a recursively computed asymptotic expansion.

</details>


### [98] [Adaptive Momentum via Minimal Dual Function for Accelerating Randomized Sparse Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29276)
*Lu Zhang,Jinchuan Zeng,Hongxia Wang,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于最小对偶函数原理的自适应动量方法，结合分位数采样构建统一框架，用于处理精确、噪声或损坏的线性系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越最小误差原理的精确测量限制，开发更通用的自适应动量方法，为处理各种类型的线性系统提供统一框架

Method: 基于最小对偶函数原理设计自适应动量方法，结合分位数采样构建quantile-based randomized sparse Kaczmarz with minimal dual function momentum框架，使用差异原理和单调误差作为停止准则

Result: 理论上建立了Bregman距离的期望线性收敛性（达到与污染水平相关的有限范围），数值实验在模拟和真实数据上验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的基于最小对偶函数原理的自适应动量方法超越了最小误差原理的限制，为处理精确、噪声或损坏的线性系统提供了有效的统一框架

Abstract: Recently, the randomized sparse Kaczmarz method has been accelerated by designing heavy ball momentum adaptively via a minimal-error principle. In this paper, we develop a new adaptive momentum method based on the minimal dual function principle to go beyond the exact measurement restriction of the minimal-error principle. Moreover, by integrating the new adaptive momentum method with the quantile-based sampling, we introduce a general algorithmic framework, called quantile-based randomized sparse Kaczmarz with minimal dual function momentum, which provides a unified approach to exact, noisy, or corrupted linear systems. In addition, we utilize the discrepancy principle and monotone error as stopping rules for the proposed algorithm. Theoretically, we establish linear convergence in expectation of Bregman distance up to a finite horizon related to the contaminated level. At last, we provide numerical illustrations on simulated and real-world data to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>


### [99] [Convergence analysis of dynamical systems for optimization by an improved Lyapunov framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29330)
*Atsushi Tabei,Ken'ichiro Tanaka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的Lyapunov函数分析方法，通过重组Lyapunov函数来评估f(x(t))-f_*-g(t)‖x(t)-x_*‖²而非f(x(t))-f_*，结合计算机辅助框架发现Lyapunov函数，获得比现有研究更好的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lyapunov函数分析在强凸函数优化方法的连续时间动力系统收敛分析中存在局限性，需要改进分析方法以获得更精确的收敛率估计。

Method: 结合Aujol-Dossal-Rondepierre (2023)提出的Lyapunov函数重组技术（评估f(x(t))-f_*-g(t)‖x(t)-x_*‖²而非f(x(t))-f_*）与计算机辅助Lyapunov函数发现框架，开发改进的收敛分析方法。

Result: 该方法能够复现现有收敛率，或获得比先前研究更好的收敛率，改进了强凸函数优化方法连续时间动力系统的收敛分析。

Conclusion: 通过Lyapunov函数重组技术与计算机辅助框架的结合，可以克服传统Lyapunov分析的局限性，为优化方法的连续时间动力系统提供更精确的收敛率分析工具。

Abstract: We study the convergence analysis of continuous-time dynamical systems associated with optimization methods for strongly convex functions. Recent works have proposed systematic constructions of Lyapunov functions for such analysis, while also revealing limitations of the Lyapunov analysis. Aujol--Dossal--Rondepierre (2023) have proposed a technique to address this issue by reorganizing Lyapunov functions so as to evaluate a quantity $f(x(t)) - f_* - g(t)\|x(t)-x_*\|^2$ rather than $f(x(t)) - f_*$. By combining this technique with our computer-assisted framework to discover Lyapunov functions, we develop an improved method that reproduces an existing convergence rate or yields better rates than previous studies.

</details>


### [100] [One-parameter Filled Function Method for Non-convex Multi-objective Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29338)
*Bikram Adhikary,Md Abu Talhamainuddin Ansary*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的单参数填充函数方法用于非线性多目标优化，避免标量化权重和先验偏好排序，通过迭代搜索逼近全局弱有效解和Pareto前沿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中现有方法通常需要标量化权重或先验偏好排序，难以处理非凸问题中多个局部Pareto前沿的情况，需要开发能够全局搜索且不依赖权重的方法

Method: 基于单目标优化中的填充函数思想，提出单参数填充函数方法：1) 使用下降过程获得局部弱有效解；2) 构造关联填充函数推导出改进的局部弱有效解；3) 重复这两个阶段驱动搜索向全局弱有效解收敛

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在一系列测试问题上相对于现有方法具有有效性，能够处理非凸问题并近似全局Pareto前沿

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性多目标优化提供了一种有效的全局搜索方法，不依赖标量化权重和先验偏好排序，特别适用于存在多个局部Pareto前沿的非凸问题

Abstract: In this paper, a new one-parameter filled function approach is developed for nonlinear multi-objective optimization. Inspired by key filled function ideas from single-objective optimization, the proposed method is adapted to the multi-objective setting. It avoids scalarization weights and does not impose any prior preference ordering among objectives. A descent-based procedure is first applied to obtain a local weak efficient solution. An associated filled function is then constructed and used to derive another local weak efficient solution that improves upon the current one. Repeating these phases drives the search toward global weak efficiency and yields an approximation of the global Pareto front, including in non-convex problems where multiple local Pareto fronts may exist. Numerical experiments on a set of test problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach relative to existing methods.

</details>


### [101] [Stochastic Block Bregman Projection with Polyak-like Stepsize for Possibly Inconsistent Convex Feasibility Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29348)
*Lu Zhang,Hongzhen Chen,Hongxia Wang,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的随机双层重构方法来解决可能不一致的凸可行性问题，结合了邻近函数最小化和结构正则化，并开发了具有Polyak-like和投影步长的随机块Bregman投影方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机投影算法在解决凸可行性问题中具有广泛应用，但现有方法在处理不一致问题和算法收敛性方面存在局限。需要开发一种统一框架来处理可能不一致的CFPs，同时保证算法的收敛性和稳定性。

Method: 提出随机双层重构框架，结合邻近函数最小化和结构正则化；开发随机块Bregman投影方法，采用Polyak-like和投影步长；该方法包含多种现有随机投影算法，并能针对特定问题生成新方案。

Result: 建立了期望内函数的遍历次线性收敛率，在Bregman距离增长条件下实现了对内部最小化器集的期望线性收敛；Polyak-like步长确保了可能不一致CFPs的精确期望收敛；数值实验验证了方法的有效性和对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一随机双层重构框架和随机块Bregman投影方法为处理可能不一致的凸可行性问题提供了有效的解决方案，具有理论收敛保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Stochastic projection algorithms for solving convex feasibility problems (CFPs) have attracted considerable attention due to their broad applicability. In this paper, we propose a unified stochastic bilevel reformulation for possibly inconsistent CFPs that combines proximity function minimization and structural regularization, leading to a feasible bilevel model with a unique and stable regularized solution. From the algorithmic perspective, we develop the stochastic block Bregman projection method with Polyak-like and projective stepsizes, which not only subsumes several recent stochastic projection algorithms but also induces new schemes tailored to specific problems. Moreover, we establish ergodic sublinear convergence rates for the expected inner function, as well as linear convergence in expectation to the inner minimizer set under a Bregman distance growth condition. In particular, the proposed Polyak-like stepsize ensures exact convergence in expectation for possibly inconsistent CFPs. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its robustness to noise.

</details>


### [102] [Stochastic homogenization of nonconvex unbounded integral functionals with generalized Orlicz growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29465)
*Davide Aruta,Francesca Prinari,Francesco Solombrino*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机积分泛函的同质化问题，这些泛函可能是无界的，其定义域可能依赖于空间变量。在向量情况下，作者为具有广义Orlicz型凸增长的非凸无界泛函建立了完整的随机同质化理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机积分泛函的同质化问题，特别是处理无界泛函（即积分核的定义域不是整个空间且可能依赖于空间变量）的情况。这类问题在材料科学和连续介质力学中具有重要应用，其中材料的微观结构具有随机性。

Method: 采用Γ-收敛的局部化方法，结合截断论证技术。研究非凸无界泛函，要求满足凸增长条件（广义Orlicz型），并假设标准条件：p->1的强制性条件和p+<∞的上界条件。

Result: 建立了完整的随机同质化理论，证明了极限能量定义在可能各向异性的Musielak-Orlicz空间中。提供了该空间中光滑函数逼近的结果，确保了理论的实际可计算性。

Conclusion: 成功发展了非凸无界随机积分泛函的随机同质化理论，为处理定义域依赖于空间变量的随机材料模型提供了数学基础，扩展了传统有界泛函同质化理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We consider the homogenization of random integral functionals which are possibly unbounded, that is, the domain of the integrand is not the whole space and may depend on the space-variable. In the vectorial case, we develop a complete stochastic homogenization theory for nonconvex unbounded functionals with convex growth of generalized Orlicz-type, under a standard set of assumptions in the field, in particular a coercivity condition of order $p^->1$, and an upper bound of order $p^+<\infty$. The limit energy is defined in a possibly anisotropic Musielak-Orlicz space, for which approximation results with smooth functions are provided. The proof is based on the localization method of $Γ$-convergence and a careful use of truncation arguments.

</details>


### [103] [A Mollification Approach to Ramified Transport and Tree Shape Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29567)
*Alberto Bressan,Giacomo Vecchiato,Ludmil Zikatanov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于数值计算最优灌溉模式的磨光算法，在拉格朗日框架下对标准灌溉成本泛函进行正则化，并应用于树根和树枝最优形状的数值优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准灌溉成本泛函在数值计算中可能存在不连续性或奇异性，需要正则化方法来实现有效的数值优化。特别是对于树根和树枝形状优化这类实际问题，需要稳定可靠的数值算法。

Method: 提出了一种磨光算法，在拉格朗日框架下对灌溉成本泛函进行正则化处理。通过数学分析证明了该正则化方法的下半连续性和Gamma收敛性，为数值计算提供理论保证。

Result: 证明了磨光算法作为正则化方法的数学性质：下半连续性和Gamma收敛性。将该技术应用于树根和树枝最优形状的数值优化问题，展示了算法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 磨光算法为灌溉成本泛函提供了有效的正则化方法，具有坚实的数学理论基础，能够成功应用于树根和树枝形状优化等实际数值优化问题。

Abstract: The paper analyzes a mollification algorithm, for the numerical computation of optimal irrigation patterns. This provides a regularization of the standard irrigation cost functional, in a Lagrangian framework. Lower semicontinuity and Gamma-convergence results are proved. The technique is then applied to some numerical optimization problems, related to the optimal shape of tree roots and branches.

</details>


### [104] [Stochastic Model Predictive Control based on Mixed Random Variables for Economic Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29586)
*Janik Pinter,Maximilian Beichter,Ralf Mikut,Veit Hagenmeyer,Frederik Zahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于混合随机变量的电池调度优化方法，通过优化电池功率区间而非固定设定点，有效处理可再生能源发电和负荷的不确定性，在住宅场景中实现最低用电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池优化调度具有降低电力成本和增强电网韧性的潜力，但需要同时考虑物理约束以及可再生能源发电和负荷的不确定性。现有方法通常优化固定的电池功率设定点，难以有效处理不确定性。

Method: 提出基于混合随机变量（离散和连续概率分布的混合）的区间优化方法。开发了分析性随机公式，用于最小化住宅场景（包含负荷、光伏和电池储能）的电力成本。优化电池功率区间而非固定设定点，允许在物理限制内明确考虑不确定性。

Result: 在15个住宅建筑连续5个月的真实数据上进行验证。与确定性和概率基准控制器相比，提出的区间优化方法实现了最低成本。混合随机变量被证明是处理不确定性决策的实用且有前景的工具。

Conclusion: 混合随机变量为不确定性下的决策提供了实用框架，通过优化电池功率区间而非固定设定点，能够有效处理可再生能源和负荷的不确定性，在住宅电池调度中实现成本最小化。

Abstract: Optimal scheduling of batteries has significant potential to reduce electricity costs and to enhance grid resilience. However, effective battery scheduling must account for both physical constraints as well as uncertainties in consumption and generation of renewable energy sources. Instead of optimizing fixed battery power setpoints, we propose an approach that optimizes battery power intervals, allowing the optimization to explicitly account for uncertain consumption and generation as well as how the battery system should respond to them within its physical limits. Our method is based on mixed random variables, represented as mixtures of discrete and continuous probability distributions. Building on this representation, we develop an analytical stochastic formulation for minimizing electricity costs in a residential setting with load, photovoltaics, and battery storage. We demonstrate its effectiveness across real-world data from 15 residential buildings over five consecutive months. Compared with deterministic and probabilistic benchmark controllers, the proposed interval-based optimization achieves the lowest costs. These results show that mixed random variables are a practical and promising tool for decision-making under uncertainty.

</details>


### [105] [Adaptive Mitigation of Insider Threats via Off-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29594)
*Gehui Xu,Kaiwen Chen,Zhong-Ping Jiang,Thomas Parisini,Andreas A. Malikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究团队协作中的内部人威胁问题，提出基于博弈论的连续时间切换系统框架，开发周期性离策略缓解方案，使决策者能在未知内部人意图的情况下从在线数据中学习最优缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部人威胁是团队协作中的严重问题，内部成员表面上合作但暗中追求私利，会降低集体性能、威胁任务成功并危及操作安全。现有方法通常假设内部人意图已知，但实际中内部人意图往往是未知且动态变化的。

Method: 采用博弈论框架，将内部人威胁建模为连续时间切换系统，每个时间间隔对应不同的内部人行为模式或威胁级别。开发周期性离策略缓解方案，使决策者能够从在线数据中学习最优缓解策略，无需先验知识。通过设计适当的学习间隔时间条件，确保学习过程和闭环系统的收敛性。

Result: 建立了学习过程和闭环系统的收敛性保证，并表征了决策者实现的缓解性能。提出的方法能够在内部人意图未知的情况下有效学习并实施缓解策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于博弈论的连续时间切换系统框架和周期性离策略缓解方案，为处理动态变化的内部人威胁提供了一种有效方法，能够在无需先验知识的情况下从在线数据中学习最优缓解策略，并保证系统的收敛性和性能。

Abstract: An insider is a team member who covertly deviates from the team's optimal collaborative strategy to pursue a private objective while still appearing cooperative. Such an insider may initially behave cooperatively but later switch to selfish or malicious actions, thereby degrading collective performance, threatening mission success, and compromising operational safety. In this paper, we study such insider threats within an insider-aware, game-theoretic formulation, where the insider interacts with a decision maker (DM) under a continuous-time switched system, with each time interval characterized by a distinct insider behavioral pattern or threat level. We develop a periodic off-policy mitigation scheme that enables the DM to learn optimal mitigation policies from online data when encountering different insider threats, without requiring a priori knowledge of insider intentions. By designing appropriate conditions on the inter-learning interval time, we establish convergence guarantees for both the learning process and the closed-loop system, and characterize the corresponding mitigation performance achieved by the DM.

</details>


### [106] [Quantitative Uniqueness of Kantorovich Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29595)
*William Ford*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究对偶最优传输问题解的唯一性，包括定性唯一性（解是否唯一）和定量唯一性（最优解集合直径的界）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对偶最优传输问题中解的唯一性条件，特别是在边际测度支撑集为低维子集或最优势函数不可微的情况下，现有理论存在局限性

Method: 1. 定性分析：证明当边际测度支撑集为可求长连通时，最优对偶势函数在常数意义下唯一；2. 定量分析：通过将最优对偶势集合表征为显式半空间族的交集，用Hausdorff距离控制最优解集合的直径

Result: 1. 首次获得适用于边际测度支撑集为低维子集的最优对偶势唯一性结果；2. 建立了最优解集合直径与边际测度支撑集连通性之间的定量关系；3. 证明了即使最优势函数在边际测度支撑集上处处不可微，唯一性仍然成立

Conclusion: 该研究为对偶最优传输问题解的唯一性提供了新的理论框架，将唯一性条件从传统的光滑连通性推广到更一般的可求长连通性，并建立了定量控制方法

Abstract: This paper studies the uniqueness of solutions to the dual optimal transport problem, both qualitatively and quantitatively (bounds on the diameter of the set of optimisers).
  On the qualitative side, we prove that when one marginal measure's support is rectifiably connected (path-connected by rectifiable paths), the optimal dual potentials are unique up to a constant. This represents the first uniqueness result applicable even when both marginal measures are concentrated on lower-dimensional subsets of the ambient space, and also applies in cases where optimal potentials are nowhere differentiable on the supports of the marginals.
  On the quantitative side, we control the diameter of the set of optimal dual potentials by the Hausdorff distance between the support of one of the marginal measures and a regular connected set. In this way, we quantify the extent to which optimisers are almost unique when the support of one marginal measure is almost connected. This is a consequence of a novel characterisation of the set of optimal dual potentials as the intersection of an explicit family of half-spaces.

</details>


### [107] [An objective-function-free algorithm for nonconvex stochastic optimization with deterministic equality and inequality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29685)
*S. Gratton,Ph. L. Toint*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于求解具有随机目标和确定性等式/不等式约束优化问题的算法，该算法无需评估目标函数值，仅使用梯度信息，结合自适应选择函数下降和约束改进迭代，在满秩雅可比矩阵条件下达到O(1/√k)收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有随机目标函数和确定性约束的优化问题，传统方法通常需要评估目标函数值。本文旨在开发一种无需函数值评估、仅使用梯度信息的算法，以降低计算成本并适应随机优化场景。

Method: 提出一种目标函数无关算法，仅使用目标函数梯度而不评估函数值。算法基于自适应选择函数下降迭代和约束改进迭代，其中函数下降迭代采用Adagrad型步长。通过结合这两种迭代类型处理约束优化问题。

Result: 当应用于具有满秩雅可比矩阵的问题时，证明原始-对偶最优性度量以O(1/√k)的速率下降，这与无约束情况下一阶方法的收敛速率相同。

Conclusion: 该算法成功实现了仅使用梯度信息而不评估目标函数值的约束随机优化，在满秩条件下达到了与无约束优化相同的最优收敛速率，为随机约束优化提供了高效的计算框架。

Abstract: An algorithm is proposed for solving optimization problems with stochastic objective and deterministic equality and inequality constraints. This algorithm is objective-function-free in the sense that it only uses the objective's gradient and never evaluates the function value. It is based on an adaptive selection of function-decreasing and constraint-improving iterations, the first ones using an Adagrad-type stepsize. When applied to problems with full-rank Jacobian, the combined primal-dual optimality measure is shown to decrease at the rate of O(1/sqrt{k}), which is identical to the convergence rate of first-order methods in the unconstrained case.

</details>


### [108] [On Lipschitzian properties of multifunctions defined implicitly by "split" feasibility problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29703)
*Amos Uderzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了参数化"分裂"可行性问题解映射的定量半连续性（Lipschitz性质），包括Lipschitz下半连续性、calmness、孤立calmness、Lipschitz上半连续性和Aubin性质，并提供了相关精确界的定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究参数化分裂可行性问题解映射的定量半连续性性质，这类问题在工程和系统生物学中有广泛应用。作为变分系统扰动分析的一部分，该研究旨在建立解映射的定量稳定性理论。

Method: 采用变分分析技术，通过凸分析构造（涉及问题数据）表达的对偶正则性条件，建立解映射各种Lipschitz性质（Lipschitz下半连续性、calmness、孤立calmness、Lipschitz上半连续性、Aubin性质）的充分条件。

Result: 获得了参数化分裂可行性问题解映射各种定量半连续性性质的充分条件，并为每种性质提供了相关精确界的定量估计。提出了统一研究这些性质的方法。

Conclusion: 通过变分分析技术和对偶正则性条件，系统建立了参数化分裂可行性问题解映射的定量稳定性理论，提供了统一的研究框架和精确的定量估计。

Abstract: In the present paper, a systematic study is made of quantitative semicontinuity (a.k.a. Lipschitzian) properties of certain multifunctions, which are defined as a solution map associated to a family of parameterized ``split" feasibility problems. The latter are a particular class of convex feasibility problems with well recognized applications to several areas of engineering and systems biology. As a part of a perturbation analysis of variational systems, this study falls within the framework of a line of research pursued by several authors. It is performed by means of techniques of variational analysis, which lead to establish sufficient conditions for the Lipschitz lower semicontinuity, calmness, isolated calmness, Lipschitz upper semicontinuity and Aubin property of the solution map. Along with each of these properties, a quantitative estimate of the related exact bound is also provided. The key elements emerging on the way to achieving the main results are dual regularity conditions qualifying the problem behaviour, which are expressed in terms of convex analysis constructions involving problem data. The approach here proposed tries to unify the study of the aforementioned properties.

</details>


### [109] [Generalizing Output-Feedback Covariance Steering to Incorporate Non-Orthogonal Estimation Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29753)
*Daniel C. Qi,Kenshiro Oguri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在输出反馈下处理有限时域状态分布控制的方法，解决了状态估计误差与状态估计非正交时带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布控制方法通常假设估计误差与状态估计正交，但在实际输出反馈系统中，这种正交性假设可能不成立。当估计误差与状态估计非正交时，会引入统计耦合问题，导致现有方法失效。

Method: 开发了一个通用框架来捕捉估计误差与状态估计的非正交性，将问题表述为可通过序列凸规划求解的形式，并处理秩约束。该方法扩展了现有分布控制公式，适用于更一般的输出反馈场景。

Result: 通过数值示例和蒙特卡洛仿真验证了所提方法的有效性，特别是在估计误差非正交的情况下，该方法能够成功处理传统技术无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了分布控制理论，为输出反馈系统中存在非正交估计误差的情况提供了有效的解决方案，具有比现有方法更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of steering a state distribution over a finite horizon in discrete time with output feedback. The incorporation of output feedback introduces additional challenges arising from the statistical coupling between the true state distribution and the corresponding filtered state distribution. In particular, this paper extends existing distribution steering formulations to scenarios in which estimation errors are not orthogonal to the state estimates. A general framework is developed to capture this non-orthogonality, and the resulting problem is formulated in a form solvable via sequential convex programming with rank constraints. The proposed approach generalizes existing methods and is validated through numerical examples and Monte Carlo simulations, including cases with non-orthogonal estimation errors that prior techniques cannot address.

</details>


### [110] [Noncooperative Game in Multi-controller System under Delayed and Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29783)
*Xin Li,Qingyuan Qi,Kemi Ding*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了多控制器系统中具有延迟和不对称信息结构的非合作博弈问题，通过信息分解方法获得闭式纳什均衡，并分析了信息不对称对博弈结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多控制器系统中，当存在延迟和不对称信息结构时，经典分离原理失效，估计与控制设计强耦合，难以推导显式纳什均衡。需要解决这一理论挑战。

Method: 采用公共-私有信息分解方法，解耦控制输入和状态估计；应用前向迭代法建立耦合的Riccati方程和估计误差协方差递推的收敛性。

Result: 获得了闭式纳什均衡解，证明了稳态卡尔曼滤波器和纳什均衡的存在；量化了信息不对称的影响，证明信息更丰富的玩家成本更低。

Conclusion: 提出的信息分解方法有效解决了延迟和不对称信息结构下的非合作博弈问题，为多控制器系统设计提供了理论框架，并通过数值算例验证了有效性。

Abstract: We address a noncooperative game problem in multi-controller system under delayed and asymmetric information structure. Under these conditions, the classical separation principle fails as estimation and control design become strongly coupled, complicating the derivation of an explicit Nash equilibrium. To resolve this, we employ a common-private information decomposition approach, effectively decoupling control inputs and state estimation to obtain a closed-form Nash equilibrium. By applying a forward iterative method, we establish the convergence of the coupled Riccati and estimation error covariance recursions, yielding both the steady-state Kalman filter and the Nash equilibrium. Furthermore, we quantify the impact of asymmetric information, proving that a richer information set reduces the costs for the corresponding player. Finally, numerical examples are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of the results.

</details>


### [111] [Analyzing Performance and Scalability of Benders Decomposition for Generation and Transmission Expansion Planning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29867)
*David L. Cole,Michael Lau,Xinliang Dai,Sambuddha Chakrabarti,Jesse D. Jenkins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在具有节点分辨率和DCOPF约束的GTEP模型中，通过多种策略加速Benders分解算法性能的方法，包括广义Benders分解、热启动、主问题线性松弛和正则化等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GTEP问题能够比传统的GEP或TEP问题提供更好的规划决策，但计算复杂度高或难以求解。Benders分解已应用于扩展规划问题，但需要加速收敛策略。

Method: 测试了多种加速Benders分解的策略组合：广义Benders分解、通过传输约束模型热启动、使用主问题线性松弛、以及正则化技术。还提出了处理双线性约束的替代方法。

Result: 在包含146个节点（1000万连续变量和400个混合整数决策）的MILP GTEP模型中，选定的加速Benders分解方法能够在5小时内求解到1%的间隙，而原本问题是难以求解的。结果表明，在初始热启动和松弛步骤中使用正则化，完成后关闭正则化是最佳策略组合。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的加速策略组合，特别是结合热启动、线性松弛和自适应正则化，可以有效解决大规模GTEP问题的计算复杂度问题，使原本难以求解的问题变得可处理。

Abstract: Generation and Transmission Expansion Planning (GTEP) problems co-optimize generation and transmission expansion, enabling them to provide better planning decisions than traditional Generation Expansion Planning or Transmission Expansion Planning problems, but GTEPs can be computationally complex or intractable. Benders Decomposition (BD) has been applied to expansion planning problems, with various methods applied to accelerate convergence. In this work, we test strategies for improving the performance of BD on GTEP models with nodal resolution and DCOPF constraints. We also present an alternative approach for handling the bilinear constraints that can result in these problems. These tests included combinations of using generalized Benders decomposition (GBD), hot-starting via a transport constrained model, using linear relaxations of the master problem, and using regularization. We test these methods on mixed-integer linear programming GTEP models with up to 146 buses (10 million continuous variables and 400 mixed-integer decisions). With selected accelerated Benders decomposition approaches, the problems can be solved to under a 1\% gap in as little as 5 hours where they were otherwise intractable. Results also suggest that using regularization on these initial hot-starting and relaxation steps and turning it off after they are complete was generally the best combination of strategies.

</details>


### [112] [Projection-Free Algorithms for Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29870)
*Khanh-Hung Giang-Tran,Soroosh Shafiee,Nam Ho-Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一的对偶动态平滑框架，解决约束光滑鞍点问题中投影计算昂贵的问题，设计高效单循环算法，缩小投影自由方法与投影方法之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束光滑鞍点问题中，当投影到可行集的计算成本很高时，需要设计既能避免昂贵投影操作又能保持高效收敛的算法。现有方法在投影自由和投影方法之间存在性能差距，需要统一框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出统一的对偶动态平滑框架，支持设计高效单循环算法。基于该框架开发三种算法变体：分别对最小化变量、最大化变量或两者应用线性最小化预言机。框架适用于非凸-凹、非凸-强凹和凸-凹问题。

Result: 建立了非凸-凹和非凸-强凹设置下的收敛结果，为凸-凹问题提供统一分析。算法具有任意时间收敛保证，无需预先指定迭代次数。显著缩小了投影自由方法与投影方法之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 提出的对偶动态平滑框架成功弥合了投影自由和投影方法之间的差距，为约束光滑鞍点问题提供了高效的单循环算法，具有广泛的适用性和理论保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses constrained smooth saddle-point problems in settings where projection onto the feasible sets is computationally expensive. We bridge the gap between projection-based and projection-free optimization by introducing a unified dual dynamic smoothing framework that enables the design of efficient single-loop algorithms. Within this framework, we establish convergence results for nonconvex-concave and nonconvex-strongly concave settings. Furthermore, we show that this framework is naturally applicable to convex-concave problems, providing a unified analysis across varying payoff structures. We propose and analyze three algorithmic variants based on the application of a linear minimization oracle over the minimization variable, the maximization variable, or both. Notably, our analysis yields anytime convergence guarantees without requiring a pre-specified iteration horizon. These results significantly narrow the performance gap between projection-free and projection-based methods for minimax optimization.

</details>


### [113] [A McKean-Pontrygin maximum principle for entropic-regularized optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30019)
*Sebastian Reich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于McKean-Pontryagin最大值原理的平均场动态最优传输方法，避免随机路径采样，采用变分方法得到约束哈密顿运动方程，统一处理确定性和随机最优传输问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种避免随机路径采样的动态最优传输方法，通过变分方法统一处理确定性和随机最优传输问题，并建立与现有方法的联系。

Method: 基于McKean-Pontryagin最大值原理的平均场方法，采用完全变分方法推导约束哈密顿运动方程，避免随机路径采样。

Result: 建立了动态最优传输的平均场方法框架，统一处理确定性和随机最优传输问题，并与前向-后向随机微分方程方法建立联系。

Conclusion: 提出的平均场方法为动态最优传输问题提供了有效的计算框架，避免了随机路径采样的复杂性，并建立了与现有方法的理论联系。

Abstract: This note outlines a mean-field approach to dynamic optimal transport problems based on the recently proposed McKean-Pontryagin maximum principle. Key aspects of the proposed methodology include i) avoidance of sampling over stochastic paths, ii) a fully variational approach leading to constrained Hamiltonian equations of motion, and iii) a unified treatment of deterministic and stochastic optimal transport problems. We also discuss connections to well-known dynamic formulations in terms of forward-backward stochastic differential equations and extensions beyond classical entropic-regularized transport problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [ChartDiff: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Comprehending Pairs of Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28902)
*Rongtian Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartDiff是首个大规模跨图表比较总结基准，包含8,541个图表对，评估发现前沿通用模型在GPT质量评估中表现最佳，但词汇重叠指标与人类评估存在明显不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要关注单图表解释，缺乏跨图表比较推理的评估，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建ChartDiff基准，包含8,541个图表对，涵盖多样数据源、图表类型和视觉风格，使用LLM生成并人工验证的差异总结。评估通用模型、图表专用模型和流水线方法。

Result: 前沿通用模型在GPT质量评估中得分最高；专用和流水线方法ROUGE分数更高但人类对齐评估较低；多序列图表对所有模型都具有挑战性；端到端模型对绘图库差异相对稳健。

Conclusion: 比较图表推理对当前视觉语言模型仍是重大挑战，ChartDiff为推进多图表理解研究提供了新基准。

Abstract: Charts are central to analytical reasoning, yet existing benchmarks for chart understanding focus almost exclusively on single-chart interpretation rather than comparative reasoning across multiple charts. To address this gap, we introduce ChartDiff, the first large-scale benchmark for cross-chart comparative summarization. ChartDiff consists of 8,541 chart pairs spanning diverse data sources, chart types, and visual styles, each annotated with LLM-generated and human-verified summaries describing differences in trends, fluctuations, and anomalies. Using ChartDiff, we evaluate general-purpose, chart-specialized, and pipeline-based models. Our results show that frontier general-purpose models achieve the highest GPT-based quality, while specialized and pipeline-based methods obtain higher ROUGE scores but lower human-aligned evaluation, revealing a clear mismatch between lexical overlap and actual summary quality. We further find that multi-series charts remain challenging across model families, whereas strong end-to-end models are relatively robust to differences in plotting libraries. Overall, our findings demonstrate that comparative chart reasoning remains a significant challenge for current vision-language models and position ChartDiff as a new benchmark for advancing research on multi-chart understanding.

</details>


### [115] [Working Paper: Towards a Category-theoretic Comparative Framework for Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28906)
*Pablo de los Riscos,Fernando J. Corbacho,Michael A. Arbib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用范畴论为通用人工智能（AGI）架构建立形式化框架，旨在统一描述、比较和分析不同AGI系统，包括强化学习、因果强化学习、基于模式学习等架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AGI研究缺乏统一的形式化定义和比较框架，各大科技公司投入巨大资源但缺乏理论基础。需要建立数学框架来明确描述不同AGI架构的共同点和差异，揭示未来研究方向。

Method: 采用范畴论方法，特别是"范畴中的机器"概念，为AGI架构提供现代视角。具体包括：1）在范畴中形式化RL、因果RL和SBL架构；2）建立统一框架，整合架构结构、信息组织、智能体实现、环境交互、行为发展和经验评估。

Result: 提出了第一个范畴论形式的AGI架构框架，能够明确比较不同AGI候选架构（如RL、通用AI、主动推理、CRL、基于模式学习等），揭示它们的共性和差异，并识别未来研究领域。

Conclusion: 范畴论与AGI将形成共生关系，该框架为AGI系统提供了统一的形式基础，支持定义架构的句法、信息和语义属性，以及智能体在明确特征环境中的评估。

Abstract: AGI has become the Holly Grail of AI with the promise of level intelligence and the major Tech companies around the world are investing unprecedented amounts of resources in its pursuit. Yet, there does not exist a single formal definition and only some empirical AGI benchmarking frameworks currently exist. The main purpose of this paper is to develop a general, algebraic and category theoretic framework for describing, comparing and analysing different possible AGI architectures. Thus, this Category theoretic formalization would also allow to compare different possible candidate AGI architectures, such as, RL, Universal AI, Active Inference, CRL, Schema based Learning, etc. It will allow to unambiguously expose their commonalities and differences, and what is even more important, expose areas for future research. From the applied Category theoretic point of view, we take as inspiration Machines in a Category to provide a modern view of AGI Architectures in a Category. More specifically, this first position paper provides, on one hand, a first exercise on RL, Causal RL and SBL Architectures in a Category, and on the other hand, it is a first step on a broader research program that seeks to provide a unified formal foundation for AGI systems, integrating architectural structure, informational organization, agent realization, agent and environment interaction, behavioural development over time, and the empirical evaluation of properties. This framework is also intended to support the definition of architectural properties, both syntactic and informational, as well as semantic properties of agents and their assessment in environments with explicitly characterized features. We claim that Category Theory and AGI will have a very symbiotic relation.

</details>


### [116] [Enhancing Policy Learning with World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28955)
*Yuci Han,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WAM是一种动作正则化的世界模型，通过联合推理未来视觉观测和驱动状态转换的动作，在DreamerV2基础上加入逆动力学目标，显著提升了CALVIN基准上8个操作任务的策略学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型仅通过图像预测进行训练，缺乏对动作相关结构的显式建模。WAM旨在通过学习动作驱动的状态转换表示，提升下游控制任务的性能。

Method: WAM在DreamerV2框架中引入逆动力学目标，预测潜在状态转换对应的动作。采用两阶段训练：首先在世界模型潜在空间上通过行为克隆预训练扩散策略，然后在冻结的世界模型中使用基于模型的PPO进行微调。

Result: 在CALVIN基准的8个操作任务上，WAM将行为克隆平均成功率从59.4%提升至71.2%。经过PPO微调后，平均成功率从79.8%提升至92.8%，其中两个任务达到100%成功率，且训练步数减少8.7倍。

Conclusion: 通过将逆动力学目标整合到世界模型中，WAM能够学习更具动作相关性的表示，显著提升策略学习效率和在复杂操作任务上的性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the World-Action Model (WAM), an action-regularized world model that jointly reasons over future visual observations and the actions that drive state transitions. Unlike conventional world models trained solely via image prediction, WAM incorporates an inverse dynamics objective into DreamerV2 that predicts actions from latent state transitions, encouraging the learned representations to capture action-relevant structure critical for downstream control. We evaluate WAM on enhancing policy learning across eight manipulation tasks from the CALVIN benchmark. We first pretrain a diffusion policy via behavioral cloning on world model latents, then refine it with model-based PPO inside the frozen world model. Without modifying the policy architecture or training procedure, WAM improves average behavioral cloning success from 59.4% to 71.2% over DreamerV2 and DiWA baselines. After PPO fine-tuning, WAM achieves 92.8% average success versus 79.8% for the baseline, with two tasks reaching 100%, using 8.7x fewer training steps.

</details>


### [117] [Mimosa Framework: Toward Evolving Multi-Agent Systems for Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28986)
*Martin Legrand,Tao Jiang,Matthieu Feraud,Benjamin Navet,Yousouf Taghzouti,Fabien Gandon,Elise Dumont,Louis-Félix Nothias*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mimosa是一个进化的多智能体框架，能够自动合成特定任务的多智能体工作流并通过实验反馈迭代优化，在ScienceAgentBench上达到43.1%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主科学研究系统受限于固定工作流和工具集，无法适应不断演化的任务和环境，需要能够动态适应和进化的框架。

Method: 采用基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的动态工具发现，通过元编排器生成工作流拓扑，使用代码生成智能体执行子任务并调用工具，通过LLM评分器提供反馈驱动工作流优化。

Result: 在ScienceAgentBench基准测试中，使用DeepSeek-V3.2达到43.1%的成功率，超越单智能体基线和静态多智能体配置，且不同模型对多智能体分解和迭代学习的响应存在异质性。

Conclusion: Mimosa的模块化架构和工具无关设计使其易于扩展，完整的执行日志和工作流存档支持可审计性，结合领域专家指导有潜力自动化跨学科的广泛科学任务。

Abstract: Current Autonomous Scientific Research (ASR) systems, despite leveraging large language models (LLMs) and agentic architectures, remain constrained by fixed workflows and toolsets that prevent adaptation to evolving tasks and environments. We introduce Mimosa, an evolving multi-agent framework that automatically synthesizes task-specific multi-agent workflows and iteratively refines them through experimental feedback. Mimosa leverages the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for dynamic tool discovery, generates workflow topologies via a meta-orchestrator, executes subtasks through code-generating agents that invoke available tools and scientific software libraries, and scores executions with an LLM-based judge whose feedback drives workflow refinement. On ScienceAgentBench, Mimosa achieves a success rate of 43.1% with DeepSeek-V3.2, surpassing both single-agent baselines and static multi-agent configurations. Our results further reveal that models respond heterogeneously to multi-agent decomposition and iterative learning, indicating that the benefits of workflow evolution depend on the capabilities of the underlying execution model. Beyond these benchmarks, Mimosa modular architecture and tool-agnostic design make it readily extensible, and its fully logged execution traces and archived workflows support auditability by preserving every analytical step for inspection and potential replication. Combined with domain-expert guidance, the framework has the potential to automate a broad range of computationally accessible scientific tasks across disciplines. Released as a fully open-source platform, Mimosa aims to provide an open foundation for community-driven ASR.

</details>


### [118] [Drop the Hierarchy and Roles: How Self-Organizing LLM Agents Outperform Designed Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28990)
*Victoria Dochkina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统在最小结构支持下能自发形成自主行为，混合协议（Sequential）比集中协调表现更好14%，自主程度随模型能力提升而增强，系统可扩展到256个智能体而不降低质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多智能体LLM系统能维持多大程度的自主性，以及什么因素促成了这种自主性。研究旨在理解在缺乏外部角色分配或设计的情况下，智能体系统能否自发形成有效的协调机制。

Method: 进行了包含25,000个任务的大规模计算实验，涵盖8个模型、4-256个智能体以及8种协调协议（从外部强加的层级结构到新兴的自组织）。实验采用混合协议（Sequential），为智能体提供最小结构支持（固定顺序），观察其自发行为。

Result: 1. 自主行为在现有LLM智能体中已出现：智能体自发发明专门角色，自愿回避能力范围外的任务，形成浅层层级结构；2. 混合协议（Sequential）比集中协调表现好14%（p<0.001）；3. 协议间质量差异达44%（Cohen's d=1.86）；4. 新兴自主程度随模型能力提升而增强；5. 系统可扩展到256个智能体而不降低质量（p=0.61）；6. 从8个智能体产生了5,006个独特角色；7. 开源模型以24倍低成本达到闭源模型95%的质量。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统在最小结构支持下能有效自组织，其自主程度与模型能力正相关。随着基础模型改进，自主协调的范围将扩大。实践启示：应为智能体提供任务、协议和有能力模型，而非预先分配角色。
